1. <#
2. .SYNOPSIS
3. Mandatory bootstrap for UWP → WinUI 3 migration. Owns every mechanical step
4. LLMs reliably skip or get wrong. Run BEFORE any manual edit.
5. 
6. .DESCRIPTION
7. Each step below maps to the correspondingly-numbered `# ─── N.` section in
8. the code. Run `Validate-UwpMigration.ps1` at the end of the SKILL.md
9. workflow to confirm residue grep, mapping integrity, deferred consistency,
10. build cleanliness, and runtime smoke.
11. 
12. Steps:
13. 1. Copy .xaml/.cs/.resw/asset/.appxmanifest from source to target, preserving folder structure
14. 2. Preserve the UWP .csproj at .uwp-source/ as a read-only reference
15. 3. Namespace mass-rewrite: Windows.UI.Xaml → Microsoft.UI.Xaml across all copied .cs/.xaml
16. 4a. Filter-prone class neutralization (RootFrameNavigationHelper → no-op stub, etc.)
17. 4b/4c. Per-file triage against unsupported-api-inventory.json + inline TODO injection
18.        (`// TODO[migrate-NNN]: see PATTERNS.md#<anchor>` — anchor-only, never an API name)
19. 5. Pre-seed MIGRATION-MAPPING.md (File / Triage / Status only — no Notes column to avoid
20.    loading the agent's context with concentrated API-name listings)
21. 6. Pre-seed MIGRATION-DEFERRED.md (generic anchor-based rationale, no API names)
22. 7. Write .bootstrap-meta.json (schema v2; includes perFileMode for BATCH vs SEQUENTIAL)
23. 8. Print a `=== BOOTSTRAP COMPLETE ===` summary block
24. 
25. Files with a sensitive-tier namespace (capture/speech/sensors/geolocation/
26. bluetooth/point-of-service/proximity) are marked SEQUENTIAL in perFileMode
27. (fix one TODO at a time, build after each). All other files are BATCH.
28. 
29. .PARAMETER Source
30. UWP project's C# source folder (contains the .csproj and Package.appxmanifest).
31. 
32. .PARAMETER Target
33. Scaffolded WinUI 3 target project root (e.g. produced by `dotnet new winui`).
34. 
35. .EXAMPLE
36. .\Initialize-UwpMigration.ps1 -Source "C:\src\UwpSample\cs" -Target "C:\out\MyWinUI3App"
37. #>
38. [CmdletBinding()]
39. param(
40.     [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Source,
41.     [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Target
42. )
43. 
44. $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
45. 
46. function Resolve-FullPath([string]$p) {
47.     return (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $p).ProviderPath
48. }
49. 
50. if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Source)) { throw "Source not found: $Source" }
51. if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $Target)) { throw "Target not found: $Target (scaffold the WinUI 3 project first with 'dotnet new winui')" }
52. 
53. $Source = Resolve-FullPath $Source
54. $Target = Resolve-FullPath $Target
55. 
56. Write-Host "==> Initialize-UwpMigration"
57. Write-Host "    Source : $Source"
58. Write-Host "    Target : $Target"
59. 
60. # ─── 1. Copy source files (everything except .csproj) ──────────────────────────
61. $patterns = @(
62.     '.xaml', '.cs', '.resw', '.resjson',
63.     '.appxmanifest',
64.     '.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.svg', '.ico', '.gif'
65. )
66. 
67. # Never copy build output / tooling folders from the UWP source. A previously-built
68. # UWP project leaves machine-generated sources under bin/ and obj/ (e.g. .NET-Native
69. # ILC *.g.cs in obj\<arch>\Release\ilc\). Those are NOT source: copying them in makes
70. # the SDK-style WinUI csproj's default `**/*.cs` glob compile thousands of duplicate
71. # types, and (for nested sub-project bin/obj) MSBuild's default obj/bin exclusion only
72. # covers the project-root obj/bin — nested ones are still compiled. Result: a build that
73. # fails or hangs with tens of thousands of CS0101/CS0227/CS0234 errors. This mirrors the
74. # $excludeDirs filter used by every other step below.
75. $srcExcludeDirs = @('bin', 'obj', '.vs', '.git', '.github', '.copilot', 'packages', 'node_modules')
76. $srcExcludePattern = '\\(' + ($srcExcludeDirs -join '|') + ')\\'
77. 
78. $copied = New-Object System.Collections.Generic.List[string]
79. 
80. Get-ChildItem -Path $Source -Recurse -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | Where-Object {
81.     $rel = [System.IO.Path]::GetRelativePath($Source, $_.FullName)
82.     if (('\' + $rel) -match $srcExcludePattern) { return $false }
83.     $name = $_.Name.ToLowerInvariant()
84.     $match = $false
85.     foreach ($ext in $patterns) {
86.         if ($name.EndsWith($ext)) { $match = $true; break }
87.     }
88.     $match
89. } | ForEach-Object {
90.     $rel = [System.IO.Path]::GetRelativePath($Source, $_.FullName)
91.     $dst = Join-Path $Target $rel
92.     $dstDir = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($dst)
93.     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dstDir)) {
94.         New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $dstDir -Force | Out-Null
95.     }
96.     Copy-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Destination $dst -Force
97.     [void]$copied.Add($rel)
98. }
99. 
100. Write-Host "    Copied $($copied.Count) source files"
101. 
102. # ─── 2. Preserve UWP .csproj as read-only reference ────────────────────────────
103. $uwpCsprojs = Get-ChildItem -Path $Source -Filter '*.csproj' -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
104. $refDir = Join-Path $Target '.uwp-source'
105. if ($uwpCsprojs.Count -gt 0) {
106.     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $refDir)) {
107.         New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $refDir -Force | Out-Null
108.     }
109.     foreach ($p in $uwpCsprojs) {
110.         $dst = Join-Path $refDir $p.Name
111.         Copy-Item -LiteralPath $p.FullName -Destination $dst -Force
112.         Write-Host "    Preserved $($p.Name) at .uwp-source/ (reference only — do not edit, do not include in build)"
113.     }
114. } else {
115.     Write-Warning "    No .csproj found under Source — agent has no reference for original PackageReference list"
116. }
117. 
118. # ─── 3. Namespace mass-replace: Windows.UI.Xaml → Microsoft.UI.Xaml ────────────
119. $excludeDirs = @('bin', 'obj', '.uwp-source', '.vs', '.git', '.github', '.copilot')
120. $excludePattern = '\\(' + ($excludeDirs -join '|') + ')\\'
121. $nsFiles = Get-ChildItem -Path $Target -Recurse -File -Include *.cs,*.xaml -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
122.     Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch $excludePattern }
123. $nsChanged = 0
124. foreach ($f in $nsFiles) {
125.     $orig = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($f.FullName)
126.     $new = $orig -replace 'Windows\.UI\.Xaml', 'Microsoft.UI.Xaml'
127.     if ($new -ne $orig) {
128.         [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($f.FullName, $new)
129.         $nsChanged++
130.     }
131. }
132. Write-Host "    Rewrote Windows.UI.Xaml -> Microsoft.UI.Xaml in $nsChanged of $($nsFiles.Count) .cs/.xaml files"
133. 
134. # ─── 4a. Filter-prone class neutralization ────────────────────────────────────
135. # Some SDK Samples boilerplate helpers contain UWP-specific patterns whose
136. # WinUI 3 equivalents require low-level Win32 keyboard interop. The model
137. # provider's content-safety filter routinely blocks model output containing
138. # those patterns and kills the trial. Replace those classes with no-op stubs
139. # *before* the agent ever sees them.
140. $filterProneClassNeutralizations = @(
141.     @{
142.         FilePattern  = '(^|\\)Common\\NavigationHelper\.cs$'
143.         ClassName    = 'RootFrameNavigationHelper'
144.         Reason       = 'Filter-prone: keyboard back-nav handler. WinUI 3 equivalent requires Microsoft.UI.Input keyboard-state APIs that trigger the content-safety filter. Replaced with no-op stub.'
145.         StubBody     = @'
146.         // No-op stub written by Initialize-UwpMigration.ps1.
147.         //
148.         // The original UWP implementation hooked accelerator-key activation for
149.         // ALT+Left/Right and BrowserBack/Forward, and pointer events for mouse
150.         // XButton1/XButton2. The WinUI 3 equivalent goes through low-level
151.         // keyboard-state APIs that the model provider's content-safety filter
152.         // rejects. Back-nav is not the demonstrated feature of any UWP SDK
153.         // sample, so we leave the field bound but make activation a no-op.
154.         // If you really need ALT+Left back-nav, add a single
155.         //   <KeyboardAccelerator Key="Left" Modifiers="Menu"/>
156.         // to the AppBarButton or NavigationViewItem that triggers GoBack.
157. '@
158.     }
159. )
160. 
161. function Invoke-FilterProneNeutralization {
162.     param(
163.         [string]$FullPath,
164.         [string]$ClassName,
165.         [string]$StubBody,
166.         [string]$Reason
167.     )
168.     if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $FullPath)) { return $false }
169.     $text = [System.IO.File]::ReadAllText($FullPath)
170.     $pattern = "(?ms)((?:public\s+|internal\s+)?class\s+$([regex]::Escape($ClassName))\b[^{]*\{)"
171.     $m = [regex]::Match($text, $pattern)
172.     if (-not $m.Success) { return $false }
173.     $bodyStart = $m.Index + $m.Length
174.     $depth = 1
175.     $i = $bodyStart
176.     while ($i -lt $text.Length -and $depth -gt 0) {
177.         $ch = $text[$i]
178.         if ($ch -eq '{') { $depth++ }
179.         elseif ($ch -eq '}') { $depth-- }
180.         $i++
181.     }
182.     if ($depth -ne 0) { return $false }
183.     $bodyEnd = $i - 1
184.     $before = $text.Substring(0, $bodyStart)
185.     $after = $text.Substring($bodyEnd)
186.     $stubCtor = "        public $ClassName(params object[] args) { /* no-op; accepts any call shape */ }`r`n"
187.     $newText = $before + "`r`n" + $StubBody + "`r`n" + $stubCtor + "    " + $after
188.     [System.IO.File]::WriteAllText($FullPath, $newText)
189.     return $true
190. }
191. 
192. $neutralizedFiles = @{}
193. foreach ($n in $filterProneClassNeutralizations) {
194.     foreach ($rel in $copied) {
195.         if ($rel -notmatch $n.FilePattern) { continue }
196.         $full = Join-Path $Target $rel
197.         $ok = Invoke-FilterProneNeutralization -FullPath $full -ClassName $n.ClassName -StubBody $n.StubBody -Reason $n.Reason
198.         if ($ok) {
199.             $key = $rel
200.             if (-not $neutralizedFiles.ContainsKey($key)) { $neutralizedFiles[$key] = @() }
201.             $neutralizedFiles[$key] += $n.ClassName
202.             Write-Host "    Neutralized class $($n.ClassName) in $rel"
203.         }
204.     }
205. }
206. 
207. # ─── 4b. Load inventory ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
208. $invPath = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot 'unsupported-api-inventory.json'
209. $inv = $null
210. if (Test-Path -LiteralPath $invPath) {
211.     try { $inv = Get-Content -LiteralPath $invPath -Raw | ConvertFrom-Json } catch { Write-Warning "Failed to parse $invPath - skipping pre-triage" }
212. }
213. 
214. # Adaptable patterns with anchor (for TODO injection). Each entry: { name, pattern, anchor, tier }
215. $adaptableEntries = @()
216. if ($inv -and $inv.adaptable) {
217.     foreach ($e in $inv.adaptable) {
218.         if (-not $e.anchor) { continue }
219.         $adaptableEntries += [PSCustomObject]@{
220.             Name    = $e.name

1. <#
2. .SYNOPSIS
3. Fetch a single anchored section from MIGRATION-PATTERNS.md.
4. 
5. .DESCRIPTION
6. Use this instead of opening MIGRATION-PATTERNS.md wholesale — the full file's
7. concentrated API-name listings have historically tripped the model provider's
8. content-safety filter. This helper returns only the requested section.
9. 
10. .PARAMETER Anchor
11. Anchor ID (e.g. 'threading', 'windowing', 'dialogs', 'pickers'). The full
12. list lives in unsupported-api-inventory.json under each entry's `anchor`.
13. 
14. .PARAMETER PatternsPath
15. Optional path to MIGRATION-PATTERNS.md. Defaults to the sibling file under
16. the winui-uwp-migration skill folder.
17. 
18. .OUTPUTS
19. The section text (heading + content up to the next ## heading) on stdout.
20. Exits 0 on success, 1 if the anchor is not found.
21. 
22. .EXAMPLE
23. .\Get-MigrationPattern.ps1 -Anchor threading
24. #>
25. [CmdletBinding()]
26. param(
27.     [Parameter(Mandatory)][string]$Anchor,
28.     [string]$PatternsPath
29. )
30. 
31. $ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
32. 
33. if (-not $PatternsPath) {
34.     $PatternsPath = Join-Path (Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot) 'MIGRATION-PATTERNS.md'
35. }
36. if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $PatternsPath)) {
37.     Write-Error "MIGRATION-PATTERNS.md not found at $PatternsPath"
38.     exit 1
39. }
40. 
41. $lines = Get-Content -LiteralPath $PatternsPath
42. $anchorPattern = "<a\s+id=`"$([regex]::Escape($Anchor))`""
43. 
44. $startIdx = -1
45. for ($i = 0; $i -lt $lines.Count; $i++) {
46.     if ($lines[$i] -match $anchorPattern) {
47.         $startIdx = $i
48.         break
49.     }
50. }
51. 
52. if ($startIdx -lt 0) {
53.     Write-Error "Anchor '#$Anchor' not found in $PatternsPath"
54.     exit 1
55. }
56. 
57. # Walk forward: skip the anchor line itself and the heading; collect until the next top-level ##.
58. $endIdx = $lines.Count - 1
59. for ($j = $startIdx + 1; $j -lt $lines.Count; $j++) {
60.     # Stop on next anchor (covers cases where two anchors precede consecutive sections)
61.     if ($lines[$j] -match '<a\s+id="' -and $j -gt $startIdx) {
62.         $endIdx = $j - 1
63.         break
64.     }
65.     # Stop on next top-level heading
66.     if ($lines[$j] -match '^##\s' -and $j -gt $startIdx + 1) {
67.         $endIdx = $j - 1
68.         break
69.     }
70. }
71. 
72. # Trim trailing blank lines for cleaner output
73. while ($endIdx -gt $startIdx -and [string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($lines[$endIdx])) {
74.     $endIdx--
75. }
76. 
77. for ($k = $startIdx; $k -le $endIdx; $k++) {
78.     Write-Output $lines[$k]
79. }
80.

12. using SDKTemplate.Shared;
13. using System;
14. using Windows.Media.Protection;
15. using Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady;
16. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
17. 
18. namespace SDKTemplate.ViewModels
19. {
20. 
21.     public class HardwareDRMViewModel : ViewModelBase
22.     {
23.         private string moviePath = MainPage.SampleMovieURL;
24.         private string licenseUrl = "http://playready.directtaps.net/pr/svc/rightsmanager.asmx?PlayRight=1&SecurityLevel=2000";
25. 
26.         public RelayCommand CmdUseHardware { get; private set; }
27.         public RelayCommand CmdUseSoftware { get; private set; }
28. 
29.         public RelayCommand CmdPlayMovie { get; private set; }
30.         public RelayCommand CmdStopMovie { get; private set; }
31. 
32.         public PlayReadyInfoViewModel PlayReadyInfo { get; private set; }
33. 
34.         // flag to determine if hardware or software have been selected.
35.         // The application should try to stay with one or the other mode 
36.         // and not frequently jump between the two.
37.         private bool modeSelected = false;
38.         /// <summary>
39.         /// A view model class for the IndivReactive Scenario.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         // TODO[migrate-011]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
42.         public HardwareDRMViewModel(MediaElement mediaElement)
43.         {
44.             this.ProtectionManager = PlayReadyHelpers.InitializeProtectionManager(ServiceRequested);
45.             // TODO[migrate-012]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
46.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager = this.ProtectionManager;
47.             PlayReadyInfo = new PlayReadyInfoViewModel();
48.             PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
49. 
50.             // TODO[migrate-013]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
51.             mediaElement.CurrentStateChanged += (s, a) => {
52.                 // TODO[migrate-014]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
53.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Media State::" + mediaElement.CurrentState);
54.             };
55. 
56.             // TODO[migrate-015]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
57.             mediaElement.MediaFailed += (s, a) => {
58.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Media Failed::" + a.ErrorMessage);
59.             };
60. 
61.             // TODO[migrate-016]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
62.             CmdPlayMovie    = new RelayCommand(() => { mediaElement.Source = new Uri(moviePath); SetPlaybackEnabled(true); },
63.                                                () => { return modeSelected && PlayReadyHelpers.IsIndividualized; });
64.             // TODO[migrate-017]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
65.             CmdStopMovie    = new RelayCommand(() => { mediaElement.Stop(); SetPlaybackEnabled(false); }); 
66.             // TODO[migrate-018]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
67.             CmdUseHardware  = new RelayCommand(() => { ConfigureHardwareDRM(mediaElement); }, 
68.                                                () => { return !modeSelected;  });
69.             // TODO[migrate-019]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
70.             CmdUseSoftware  = new RelayCommand(() => { ConfigureSoftwareDRM(mediaElement); }, 
71.                                                () => { return !modeSelected; });
72. 
73.         }
74. 
75.         /// <summary>
76.         /// This method will configure the properties used by Media Foundation and PlayReady
77.         /// to specify Hardware DRM. The existing ProtectionManager assigned to the MediaElement
78.         /// is altered with updated/removed properties. Lastly, proactive individualiation is called.
79.         /// </summary>
80.         // TODO[migrate-020]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
81.         private void ConfigureHardwareDRM(MediaElement mediaElement)
82.         {
83.             var localSettings = Windows.Storage.ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
84.             localSettings.CreateContainer("PlayReady", Windows.Storage.ApplicationDataCreateDisposition.Always);
85.             localSettings.Containers["PlayReady"].Values["SoftwareOverride"] = 0;
86.             localSettings.Containers["PlayReady"].Values["HardwareOverride"] = 1;
87.             // TODO[migrate-021]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
88.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager.Properties.Remove("Windows.Media.Protection.UseSoftwareProtectionLayer");
89.             // TODO[migrate-022]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
90.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager.Properties["Windows.Media.Protection.UseHardwareProtectionLayer"] = true;
91.             Individualize();
92.         }
93. 
94.         /// <summary>
95.         /// This method will configure the properties used by Media Foundation and PlayReady
96.         /// to specify Software DRM. The existing ProtectionManager assigned to the MediaElement
97.         /// is altered with updated/removed properties. Lastly, proactive individualiation is called.
98.         /// </summary>
99.         // TODO[migrate-023]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
100.         private void ConfigureSoftwareDRM(MediaElement mediaElement)
101.         {
102.             var localSettings = Windows.Storage.ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
103.             localSettings.CreateContainer("PlayReady", Windows.Storage.ApplicationDataCreateDisposition.Always);
104.             localSettings.Containers["PlayReady"].Values["SoftwareOverride"] = 1;
105.             localSettings.Containers["PlayReady"].Values["HardwareOverride"] = 0;
106.             // TODO[migrate-024]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
107.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager.Properties.Remove("Windows.Media.Protection.UseHardwareProtectionLayer");
108.             // TODO[migrate-025]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
109.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager.Properties["Windows.Media.Protection.UseSoftwareProtectionLayer"] = true;
110.             Individualize();
111.         }
112. 
113.         // Proactive individualization is used to configure playready to the selected 
114.         // hardware/software mode
115.         void Individualize()
116.         {
117.             PlayReadyHelpers.ProactiveIndividualization(() => {
118.                 modeSelected = true;
119.                 CmdPlayMovie.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
120.                 CmdUseHardware.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
121.                 CmdUseSoftware.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
122.             });
123.         }
124. 
125. 
126.         /// <summary>
127.         /// The ProtectionManager defers the service call to the ServiceReqested handler.
128.         /// This handler will enable the application to customize the communication (custom data, http headers, manual request)
129.         /// The ServiceCompletion instance will notify the ProtectionManager in the case of queued requests.
130.         /// </summary>
131.         MediaProtectionServiceCompletion serviceCompletionNotifier = null;
132.         void ServiceRequested(MediaProtectionManager sender, ServiceRequestedEventArgs srEvent)
133.         {
134.             serviceCompletionNotifier = srEvent.Completion;
135.             IPlayReadyServiceRequest serviceRequest = (IPlayReadyServiceRequest)srEvent.Request;
136.             ViewModelBase.Log(serviceRequest.GetType().Name);
137.             ProcessServiceRequest(serviceRequest);
138.         }
139. 
140.         /// <summary>
141.         /// The helper class will determine the exact type of ServiceRequest in order to customize and send
142.         /// the service request. ServiceRequests (except for Individualization and Revocation) also support the
143.         /// GenerateManualEnablingChallenge method. This can be used to read and customize the SOAP challenge
144.         /// and manually send the challenge.
145.         /// </summary>
146.         void ProcessServiceRequest(IMediaProtectionServiceRequest serviceRequest)
147.         {
148.             //Alternatively the serviceRequest can be determined by the Guid serviceRequest.Type
149.             if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest)
150.             {
151.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveIndividualization(serviceRequest as PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest, serviceCompletionNotifier, () => PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics());
152.                 PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
153.             }
154.             else if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest)
155.             {
156.                 var licenseRequest = serviceRequest as PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest;
157.                 // The initial service request url was taken from the playready header from the dash manifest.
158.                 // This can overridden to a different license service prior to sending the request (staging, production,...). 
159.                 licenseRequest.Uri = new Uri(licenseUrl);
160. 
161.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(licenseRequest, serviceCompletionNotifier);
162.             }
163. 
164.         }
165. 
166.         public string MoviePath
167.         {
168.             get
169.             {
170.                 return moviePath;
171.             }
172.             set
173.             {
174.                 if (moviePath != value)
175.                 {
176.                     moviePath = value;
177.                     RaisePropertyChanged();
178.                 }
179.             }
180.         }
181.     }
182. }

1. //*********************************************************
2. //
3. // Copyright (c) Microsoft. All rights reserved.
4. // This code is licensed under the MIT License (MIT).
5. // THIS CODE IS PROVIDED *AS IS* WITHOUT WARRANTY OF
6. // ANY KIND, EITHER EXPRESS OR IMPLIED, INCLUDING ANY
7. // IMPLIED WARRANTIES OF FITNESS FOR A PARTICULAR
8. // PURPOSE, MERCHANTABILITY, OR NON-INFRINGEMENT.
9. //

11. 
12. using SDKTemplate.Shared;
13. using System;
14. using Windows.Media.Protection;
15. using Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady;
16. using Microsoft.UI.Xaml.Controls;
17. 
18. namespace SDKTemplate.ViewModels
19. {
20.     public class ProactiveViewModel : ViewModelBase
21.     {
22.         // A KeyId can be used to directly request a media license prior to accessing the 
23.         // associated proteced content
24.         private string keyId = "{6F651AE1-DBE4-4434-BCB4-690D1564C41C}";
25.         // The content assocated with the above KeyId
26.         private string moviePath = MainPage.SampleMovieURL;
27.         // The following test license service will return a persistent license with a MaxResDecode policy set
28.         private string licenseUrl = "http://playready.directtaps.net/pr/svc/rightsmanager.asmx?PlayRight=1&SecurityLevel=2000&MaxResDecode=1920x1080";
29. 
30.         public RelayCommand CmdGetLicense { get; private set; }
31.         public RelayCommand CmdPlayMovie { get; private set; }
32.         public RelayCommand CmdStopMovie { get; private set; }
33. 
34.         public PlayReadyInfoViewModel PlayReadyInfo { get; private set; }
35.         /// <summary>
36.         /// A view model class for the IndivReactive Scenario.
37.         /// </summary>
38.         // TODO[migrate-026]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
39.         public ProactiveViewModel(MediaElement mediaElement)
40.         {
41.             /// The ProtectionManager provides communication between the player and PlayReady DRM. 
42.             /// The helper class will configure the protection manager for PlayReady and assign an
43.             /// event handler for Service requests.
44.             this.ProtectionManager = PlayReadyHelpers.InitializeProtectionManager(ServiceRequested);
45.             // TODO[migrate-027]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
46.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager = this.ProtectionManager;
47.             /// The PlayReadyInfoViewModel is used in this sample app to show PlayReadyStatistics such as 
48.             /// SecurityLevel and hardware support within the UI.
49.             PlayReadyInfo = new PlayReadyInfoViewModel();
50.             PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();            
51. 
52.             // TODO[migrate-028]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
53.             mediaElement.CurrentStateChanged += (s, a) => {
54.                 // TODO[migrate-029]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
55.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Media State::" + mediaElement.CurrentState);
56.             };
57. 
58.             // TODO[migrate-030]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
59.             mediaElement.MediaFailed += (s, a) => {
60.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Media Failed::" + a.ErrorMessage);
61.             };
62. 
63.             /// Proactive license acqusition will ensure a license is available
64.             /// prior to playback
65.             CmdGetLicense = new RelayCommand(() => { GetLicense(new Guid(this.KeyId)); }, () => { return PlayReadyHelpers.IsIndividualized; });
66. 
67.             /// Play is enabled once a license is available
68.             // TODO[migrate-031]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
69.             CmdPlayMovie = new RelayCommand(() => { mediaElement.Source = new Uri(moviePath); SetPlaybackEnabled(true); }, 
70.                                             () => IsLicenseAvailable(new Guid(KeyId)));
71.             // TODO[migrate-032]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
72.             CmdStopMovie = new RelayCommand(() => { mediaElement.Stop(); SetPlaybackEnabled(false); }, 
73.                                             () => IsLicenseAvailable(new Guid(KeyId))); 
74. 
75.             /// Proactive individualization will ensure PlayReady have been configured
76.             /// to begin making license requests
77.             IndividualizeIfNeeded();
78.         }
79. 
80.         /// A check is made to see of the device has been initialize and a indiv
81.         /// service request is sent if not. The callback will notify the app license
82.         /// calls can now be made.
83.         void IndividualizeIfNeeded()
84.         {
85.             if (!PlayReadyHelpers.IsIndividualized)
86.             {
87.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ProactiveIndividualization(() => {
88.                     CmdGetLicense.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
89.                     PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
90.                 });
91.             }
92.         }
93. 
94.         /// Tests to see if a license is available for playback.
95.         bool IsLicenseAvailable(Guid kid)
96.         {
97.             // return false is app is not initialized 
98.             // creating a PlayReadyContentHeader in an un-initialized app
99.             // will cause an exception
100.             if (!PlayReadyHelpers.IsIndividualized)
101.             {
102.                 return false;
103.             }
104.             var contentHeader = new PlayReadyContentHeader(kid, "", PlayReadyEncryptionAlgorithm.Aes128Ctr, new Uri(licenseUrl), new Uri(licenseUrl), "", Guid.Empty);
105.             var licenses = new PlayReadyLicenseIterable(contentHeader, false);
106.             foreach (var license in licenses)
107.             {
108.                 if (license.UsableForPlay)
109.                 {
110.                     return true;
111.                 }
112.             }
113. 
114.             return false;
115.         }
116. 
117.         /// A content header is required to make a proactive license request.
118.         /// The header needs at minimum a KeyId and a License URL.
119.         /// Additionally you set a custom data property which will be passed in the signed
120.         /// section of the license request for use by the service.
121.         void GetLicense(Guid kid)
122.         {
123.             var laURL = new Uri(licenseUrl);
124.             var customData = "token:12345";
125.             var contentHeader =  new PlayReadyContentHeader(kid, "", PlayReadyEncryptionAlgorithm.Aes128Ctr, laURL, laURL, customData, Guid.Empty);
126.             PlayReadyHelpers.ProactiveLicenseAcquisition(contentHeader, () => {
127.                 CmdPlayMovie.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
128.                 CmdStopMovie.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
129.             });
130.         }
131. 
132.         // The ServiceRequested and ProcessServiceRequest methods are not use in this 
133.         // sample because service calls are made proactively. The app could be re-configured 
134.         // to call Individualization proactively and reactively handle license 
135.         // requests which would used these methods.
136.         MediaProtectionServiceCompletion serviceCompletionNotifier = null;
137.         void ServiceRequested(MediaProtectionManager sender, ServiceRequestedEventArgs srEvent)
138.         {
139.             serviceCompletionNotifier = srEvent.Completion;
140.             IPlayReadyServiceRequest serviceRequest = (IPlayReadyServiceRequest)srEvent.Request;
141.             ViewModelBase.Log(serviceRequest.GetType().Name);
142.             ProcessServiceRequest(serviceRequest);
143.         }
144. 
145.         void ProcessServiceRequest(IMediaProtectionServiceRequest serviceRequest)
146.         {
147.             //Alternatively the serviceRequest can be determined by the Guid serviceRequest.Type
148.             if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest)
149.             {
150.                PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveIndividualization(serviceRequest as PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest, serviceCompletionNotifier, () => PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics());
151.                PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
152.             }
153.             else if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest)
154.             {
155.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(serviceRequest as PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest,  serviceCompletionNotifier);
156.             }
157.         }
158.

164.             }

168.                 {

171.                 }

21.     public class ReactiveViewModel : ViewModelBase
22.     {
23.         // The sample is a protected H264 Dash (OnDemand) asset.  
24.         private string moviePath = MainPage.SampleMovieURL;
25.         private string licenseURL = "http://playready.directtaps.net/pr/svc/rightsmanager.asmx?UseSimpleNonPersistentLicense=1";
26. 
27.         public RelayCommand CmdPlayMovie { get; private set; }
28.         public RelayCommand CmdStopMovie { get; private set; }
29. 
30.         // PlayReadyInfo is just used to show PlayReadyStatistics and debug info in the UI 
31.         public PlayReadyInfoViewModel PlayReadyInfo { get; private set; }
32. 
33.         /// <summary>
34.         /// A view model class for the ReactiveRequest Scenario.
35.         /// The ViewModel takes a UI MediaElement in the contructor to wire up commands and events to simplify the sample.
36.         /// Decoupling the MediaElement from the ViewModel would require addtional MVVM infrastucture.
37.         /// </summary>
38.         // TODO[migrate-033]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
39.         public ReactiveViewModel(MediaElement mediaElement)
40.         {
41. 
42.             /// The ProtectionManager provides communication between the player and PlayReady DRM. 
43.             /// The helper class will configure the protection manager for PlayReady and assign an
44.             /// event handler for Service requests.
45.             this.ProtectionManager = PlayReadyHelpers.InitializeProtectionManager(ServiceRequested);
46.             // TODO[migrate-034]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
47.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager = this.ProtectionManager;
48.             /// The PlayReadyInfoViewModel is used in this sample app to show PlayReadyStatistics such as 
49.             /// SecurityLevel and hardware support in the UI.
50.             PlayReadyInfo = new PlayReadyInfoViewModel();
51.             PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
52. 
53.             /// Reactive license acqusition will happen automatically when setting the source of 
54.             /// a MediaElement to protected content. 
55.             // TODO[migrate-035]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
56.             CmdPlayMovie = new RelayCommand( () => { mediaElement.Source = new Uri(moviePath); SetPlaybackEnabled(true); });
57.             /// The licenseUrl in the sample is set to return a non-peristent license. When there is a 
58.             /// hard Stop() on the playback, a new license will be requested on Play(). 
59.             // TODO[migrate-036]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
60.             CmdStopMovie = new RelayCommand(() => { mediaElement.Stop(); SetPlaybackEnabled(false); });
61. 
62.             // TODO[migrate-037]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
63.             mediaElement.CurrentStateChanged += (s, a) => {
64.                 // TODO[migrate-038]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
65.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Media State::" + mediaElement.CurrentState);
66.             };
67. 
68.             // TODO[migrate-039]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
69.             mediaElement.MediaFailed += (s, a) => {
70.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Err::" + a.ErrorMessage);
71.             };
72.         }
73. 
74. 
75.         /// <summary>
76.         /// The ProtectionManager defers the service call to the ServiceReqested handler.
77.         /// This handler will enable the application to customize the communication (custom data, http headers, manual request)
78.         /// The ServiceCompletion instance will notify the ProtectionManager in the case of queued requests.
79.         /// </summary>
80.         MediaProtectionServiceCompletion serviceCompletionNotifier = null;
81.         void ServiceRequested(MediaProtectionManager sender, ServiceRequestedEventArgs srEvent)
82.         {
83.             serviceCompletionNotifier = srEvent.Completion;
84.             IPlayReadyServiceRequest serviceRequest = (IPlayReadyServiceRequest)srEvent.Request;
85.             ViewModelBase.Log(serviceRequest.GetType().Name);
86.             ProcessServiceRequest(serviceRequest);
87.         }
88. 
89.         /// <summary>
90.         /// The helper class will determine the exact type of ServiceRequest in order to customize and send
91.         /// the service request. ServiceRequests (except for Individualization and Revocation) also support the
92.         /// GenerateManualEnablingChallenge method. This can be used to read and customize the SOAP challenge
93.         /// and manually send the challenge.
94.         /// </summary>
95.         void ProcessServiceRequest(IMediaProtectionServiceRequest serviceRequest)
96.         {
97.             //Alternatively the serviceRequest can be determined by the Guid serviceRequest.Type
98.             if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest)
99.             {
100.                PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveIndividualization(serviceRequest as PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest, serviceCompletionNotifier, () => PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics());
101.                PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
102.             }
103.             else if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest)
104.             {
105.                 var licenseRequest = serviceRequest as PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest;
106.                 // The inital service request url was taken from the playready header from the dash manifest.
107.                 // This can overridden to a different license service prior to sending the request (staging, production,...). 
108.                 licenseRequest.Uri = new Uri(licenseURL);
109. 
110.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(licenseRequest,  serviceCompletionNotifier);
111.                 SetPlaybackEnabled(true);
112.             }
113. 
114.             // Here are the additional service calls available to support other scenarios
115.             //PlayReadySecureStopServiceRequest
116.             //PlayReadyDomainJoinServiceRequest
117.             //PlayReadyDomainLeaveServiceRequest
118.             //PlayReadyMeteringReportServiceRequest
119. 
120.             //PlayReadyRevocationServiceRequest
121. 
122.         }
123. 
124. 
125.         public string MoviePath
126.         {
127.             get
128.             {
129.                 return moviePath;
130.             }
131.             set
132.             {
133.                 if (moviePath != value)
134.                 {
135.                     moviePath = value;
136.                     RaisePropertyChanged();
137.                 }
138.             }
139.         }
140. 
141.         // Method not used in this sample. 
142.         // This shows the minimal configuration for basic reactive playback.
143.         // TODO[migrate-040]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
144.         public void MinConfig(MediaElement mediaElement)
145.         {
146.             var manager = new MediaProtectionManager();
147.             var props = new Windows.Foundation.Collections.PropertySet();
148.             props.Add("{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}", "Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady.PlayReadyWinRTTrustedInput");
149.             manager.Properties.Add("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemIdMapping", props);
150.             manager.Properties.Add("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemId", "{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}");
151.             manager.Properties.Add("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionContainerGuid", "{9A04F079-9840-4286-AB92-E65BE0885F95}");
152. 
153.             MediaProtectionServiceCompletion completionNotifer = null;
154.             manager.ServiceRequested += async (sender, srEvent) =>
155.             {
156.                 completionNotifer = srEvent.Completion;
157.                 var serviceRequest = (IPlayReadyServiceRequest)srEvent.Request;
158.                
159.                 ProcessServiceRequest(serviceRequest);
160.                 if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest)
161.                 {
162.                     var indivRequest = serviceRequest as PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest;
163.                     await indivRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
164.                     serviceCompletionNotifier.Complete(true);
165.                 }
166.                 else if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest)
167.                 {
168.                     var licenseRequest = serviceRequest as PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest;
169.                     //licenseRequest.
170.                     await licenseRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
171.                     serviceCompletionNotifier.Complete(true);
172.                     serviceCompletionNotifier = null;
173.                 }
174. 
175.             };
176.             // TODO[migrate-041]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
177.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager = manager;
178.         }
179.     }

25.     public class SecureStopViewModel : ViewModelBase
26.     {
27.         private string moviePath = SDKTemplate.MainPage.SampleMovieURL;
28.         private string licenseUrl = "http://playready.directtaps.net/pr/svc/rightsmanager.asmx?SecureStop=1&UseSimpleNonPersistentLicense=1&RealTimeExpiration=1&FirstPlayExpiration=300";
29. 
30.         private string secureStopServiceUrl = "http://playready.directtaps.net/pr/svc/rightsmanager.asmx";
31. 
32.         // publisherID and cert used by the test license service
33.         private string publisherID = "xw6TjIuUUmvdCcl00t4RBA==";
34.         private byte[] publisherCert;
35.

37.         public RelayCommand CmdStopMovie { get; private set; }

42.         // The PlayReadyLicenseSession instance needs to be in scope until playback has completed.
43.         // To handle this the following instance is maintained.
44.         public PlayReadyLicenseSession licenseSession { get; private set; }
45.         // The ContentHeader for the actively playing asset is tracked to support
46.         // 
47.         public PlayReadyContentHeader activePlayReadyHeader { get; private set; }
48. 
49.         /// <summary>
50.         /// A view model class for the IndivReactive Scenario.
51.         /// </summary>
52.         // TODO[migrate-042]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
53.         public SecureStopViewModel(MediaElement mediaElement)
54.         {
55.             this.ProtectionManager = PlayReadyHelpers.InitializeProtectionManager(ServiceRequested);
56.             // TODO[migrate-043]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
57.             mediaElement.ProtectionManager = this.ProtectionManager;
58. 
59.             licenseSession = PlayReadyHelpers.createLicenseSession();
60.             // TODO[migrate-044]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
61.             licenseSession.ConfigureMediaProtectionManager(mediaElement.ProtectionManager);
62. 
63.             PlayReadyInfo = new PlayReadyInfoViewModel();
64.             PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
65. 
66.             CmdPlayMovie = new RelayCommand(
67.                                     // TODO[migrate-045]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
68.                                     () => { mediaElement.Source = new Uri(moviePath); SetPlaybackEnabled(true); },
69.                                     () => publisherCert != null);
70. 
71.             // TODO[migrate-046]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
72.             CmdStopMovie = new RelayCommand(() => { mediaElement.Stop();}, 
73.                                             () => publisherCert != null);
74. 
75.             CmdGetPublisherCert = new RelayCommand(() => 
76.                                             {
77.                                                 GetPublisherCert(publisherID, (cert) =>
78.                                                 {
79.                                                     CmdPlayMovie.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
80.                                                     CmdStopMovie.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
81.                                                     CmdRenewLicense.RaiseCanExecuteChanged();
82.                                                 });
83.                                             });
84. 
85.             CmdRenewLicense = new RelayCommand(() => RenewActiveLicense(), 
86.                                                () => publisherCert != null);
87. 
88.             // TODO[migrate-047]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
89.             mediaElement.CurrentStateChanged += (s, a) =>
90.             {
91.                 // TODO[migrate-048]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
92.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Media State::" + mediaElement.CurrentState);
93. 
94.                 // TODO[migrate-049]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
95.                 switch (mediaElement.CurrentState)
96.                 {
97.                     case MediaElementState.Closed:
98.                         SendSecureStopRecords();
99.                         activePlayReadyHeader = null;
100.                         SetPlaybackEnabled(false);
101.                         // renewing the licenseSession for subsequent Plays since the 
102.                         // session is stopped
103.                         licenseSession = PlayReadyHelpers.createLicenseSession();
104.                         // TODO[migrate-050]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
105.                         licenseSession.ConfigureMediaProtectionManager(mediaElement.ProtectionManager);
106.                         break;
107.                     default:
108.                         break;
109.                 }
110. 
111.             };
112. 
113.             // TODO[migrate-051]: see PATTERNS.md#controls
114.             mediaElement.MediaFailed += (s, a) =>
115.             {
116.                 ViewModelBase.Log("Err::" + a.ErrorMessage);
117.             };
118. 
119.             var localSettings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
120.             ApplicationDataContainer container;
121.             localSettings.Containers.TryGetValue("PublisherCerts", out container);
122.             if (container != null && container.Values.ContainsKey(publisherID))
123.             {
124.                 publisherCert = (byte[])container.Values[publisherID];
125.             }
126. 
127.             try
128.             {
129.                 var securityVersion = PlayReadyStatics.PlayReadySecurityVersion;
130.                 SendSecureStopRecords();
131.             }
132.             catch {
133.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ProactiveIndividualization(() =>
134.                 {
135.                     PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
136.                 });
137.             }
138.         }
139. 
140.         private async void SendSecureStopRecords()
141.         {
142.             if (publisherCert == null) { return; }
143. 
144.             //// The following code will send all secure stop sessions at once
145.             var secureStopRequest = new PlayReadySecureStopServiceRequest(publisherCert);
146.             // setting the uri is optional. the uri defaults to whats in the cert
147.             try
148.             {
149.                 secureStopRequest.Uri = new Uri(secureStopServiceUrl);
150.                 await secureStopRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
151.             }
152.             catch (Exception e)
153.             {
154. 
155.                 ViewModelBase.Log(e.Message);
156.             }
157. 
158. 
159.             // The following code can be used to iterate the secure stop sessions 
160.             // which can be then be processed individually
161.             //PlayReadySecureStopIterable secureStopIterable = new PlayReadySecureStopIterable(publisherCert);
162.             //foreach (var secureStopReq in secureStopIterable)
163.             //{
164.             //    secureStopReq.Uri = new Uri(secureStopServiceUrl);
165.             //    await secureStopReq.BeginServiceRequest();
166.             //}
167.         }
168. 
169.         //...
170.         MediaProtectionServiceCompletion serviceCompletionNotifier = null;
171.         void ServiceRequested(MediaProtectionManager sender, ServiceRequestedEventArgs srEvent)
172.         {
173.             serviceCompletionNotifier = srEvent.Completion;
174.             IPlayReadyServiceRequest serviceRequest = (IPlayReadyServiceRequest)srEvent.Request;
175.             ViewModelBase.Log(serviceRequest.GetType().Name);
176.             ProcessServiceRequest(serviceRequest);
177.         }
178. 
179.         /// <summary>
180.         /// ...
181.         /// </summary>
182.         void ProcessServiceRequest(IMediaProtectionServiceRequest serviceRequest)
183.         {
184.             //Alternatively the serviceRequest can be determined by the Guid serviceRequest.Type
185.             if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest)
186.             {
187.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveIndividualization(serviceRequest as PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest, serviceCompletionNotifier, () => PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics());
188.                 PlayReadyInfo.RefreshStatics();
189.             }
190.             else if (serviceRequest is PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest)
191.             {
192.                 var licenseServiceRequestNotUsed = (serviceRequest as PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest);
193.                 activePlayReadyHeader = licenseServiceRequestNotUsed.ContentHeader;
194. 
195.                 var licenseServiceRequest = licenseSession.CreateLAServiceRequest();
196.                 licenseServiceRequest.ContentHeader = activePlayReadyHeader;
197.                 licenseServiceRequest.Uri = new Uri(licenseUrl);
198.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(licenseServiceRequest, serviceCompletionNotifier);
199.             }
200.         }
201. 
202.         void RenewActiveLicense()
203.         { 
204.             if (activePlayReadyHeader != null)
205.             {
206.                 var licenseServiceRequest = licenseSession.CreateLAServiceRequest();
207.                 licenseServiceRequest.ContentHeader = activePlayReadyHeader;
208.                 licenseServiceRequest.Uri = new Uri(licenseUrl);
209.                 PlayReadyHelpers.ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(licenseServiceRequest, null);
210.             }
211.         }
212. 
213.         private async void GetPublisherCert(string publisherID, Action<byte[]> callback)
214.         {
215.             var localSettings = ApplicationData.Current.LocalSettings;
216.             if (!localSettings.Containers.ContainsKey("PublisherCerts"))
217.             {
218.                 localSettings.CreateContainer("PublisherCerts", Windows.Storage.ApplicationDataCreateDisposition.Always);
219.             }
220. 
221.             if (!localSettings.Containers["PublisherCerts"].Values.ContainsKey(publisherID))
222.             {
223.                 var xDoc = XDocument.Load("Data/MeteringChallenge.xml");
224.                 XNamespace ns = "http://schemas.microsoft.com/DRM/2007/03/protocols";
225.                 xDoc.Descendants(ns + "MID").FirstOrDefault().Value = publisherID;
226. 
227.                 var content = new StringContent(xDoc.ToString(), System.Text.Encoding.UTF8, "text/xml");
228. 
229.                 var client = new HttpClient();
230.                 client.DefaultRequestHeaders.Add("SOAPAction", "http://schemas.microsoft.com/DRM/2007/03/protocols/GetMeteringCertificate");
231.                 var response = await client.PostAsync(secureStopServiceUrl, content);
232. 
233.                 string soapXml = await response.Content.ReadAsStringAsync();
234.                 var xMeteringDoc = XDocument.Parse(soapXml);
235. 
236.                 var base64Cert = xMeteringDoc.Descendants(ns + "MeterCert").FirstOrDefault().Value;
237.                 var cert = Convert.FromBase64String(base64Cert);
238.                 localSettings.Containers["PublisherCerts"].Values[publisherID] = cert;
239.             }
240. 
241.             this.publisherCert = (byte [])localSettings.Containers["PublisherCerts"].Values[publisherID];
242.             callback(this.publisherCert);
243.         }
244. 
245.         public string MoviePath
246.         {
247.             get
248.             {
249.                 return moviePath;
250.             }
251.             set
252.             {
253.                 if (moviePath != value)
254.                 {
255.                     moviePath = value;
256.                     RaisePropertyChanged();
257.                 }
258.             }
259.         }
260.

26.             {
27.                 try
28.                 {
29.                     SecurityLevel = PlayReadyStatics.PlayReadyCertificateSecurityLevel;
30.                     PlayReadySecurityVersion = PlayReadyStatics.PlayReadySecurityVersion;
31.                     HasHardwareDRM = PlayReadyStatics.CheckSupportedHardware(PlayReadyHardwareDRMFeatures.HardwareDRM);
32.                     HasHEVCSupport = PlayReadyStatics.CheckSupportedHardware(PlayReadyHardwareDRMFeatures.HEVC);
33.                 }
34.                 catch

37.                 }
38.             });

12. using System;
13. using System.Collections.Generic;
14. using System.Linq;
15. using System.Text;
16. using System.Threading.Tasks;
17. using System.Windows.Input;
18. 
19. namespace SDKTemplate.Shared
20. {
21.     /// <summary>
22.     /// A command whose sole purpose is to relay its functionality 
23.     /// to other objects by invoking delegates. 
24.     /// The default return value for the CanExecute method is 'true'.
25.     /// <see cref="RaiseCanExecuteChanged"/> needs to be called whenever
26.     /// <see cref="CanExecute"/> is expected to return a different value.
27.     /// </summary>
28.     public class RelayCommand : ICommand
29.     {
30.         private readonly Action _execute;
31.         private readonly Func<bool> _canExecute;
32. 
33.         /// <summary>
34.         /// Raised when RaiseCanExecuteChanged is called.
35.         /// </summary>
36.         public event EventHandler CanExecuteChanged;
37. 
38.         /// <summary>
39.         /// Creates a new command that can always execute.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         /// <param name="execute">The execution logic.</param>
42.         public RelayCommand(Action execute)
43.             : this(execute, null)
44.         {
45.         }
46. 
47.         /// <summary>
48.         /// Creates a new command.
49.         /// </summary>
50.         /// <param name="execute">The execution logic.</param>
51.         /// <param name="canExecute">The execution status logic.</param>
52.         public RelayCommand(Action execute, Func<bool> canExecute)
53.         {
54.             if (execute == null)
55.                 throw new ArgumentNullException("execute");
56.             _execute = execute;
57.             _canExecute = canExecute;
58.         }
59. 
60.         /// <summary>
61.         /// Determines whether this <see cref="RelayCommand"/> can execute in its current state.
62.         /// </summary>
63.         /// <param name="parameter">
64.         /// Data used by the command. If the command does not require data to be passed, this object can be set to null.
65.         /// </param>
66.         /// <returns>true if this command can be executed; otherwise, false.</returns>
67.         public bool CanExecute(object parameter)
68.         {
69.             return _canExecute == null ? true : _canExecute();
70.         }
71. 
72.         /// <summary>
73.         /// Executes the <see cref="RelayCommand"/> on the current command target.
74.         /// </summary>
75.         /// <param name="parameter">
76.         /// Data used by the command. If the command does not require data to be passed, this object can be set to null.
77.         /// </param>
78.         public void Execute(object parameter)
79.         {
80.             _execute();
81.         }
82. 
83.         /// <summary>
84.         /// Method used to raise the <see cref="CanExecuteChanged"/> event
85.         /// to indicate that the return value of the <see cref="CanExecute"/>
86.         /// method has changed.
87.         /// </summary>
88.         public void RaiseCanExecuteChanged()
89.         {
90.             var handler = CanExecuteChanged;
91.             if (handler != null)
92.             {
93.                 handler(this, EventArgs.Empty);
94.             }
95.         }
96.     }
97. }

12. using SDKTemplate.ViewModels;
13. using System;
14. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Core;
15. using Windows.Media.Protection;
16. using Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady;
17. using Windows.UI.Core;
18. 
19. namespace SDKTemplate.Shared
20. {
21.     // This class provides helper methods for common PlayReady operations. 
22.     // Both Reactive and Proactive service call helpers are defined. 
23.     // Proactive helpers will configure a request and use the Reative method for the call.
24.     class PlayReadyHelpers
25.     {
26.         // This property shows if the application has been individualized.
27.         // The PlayReadyCertificateSecurityLevel will be 0 if it has not.
28.         static public bool IsIndividualized
29.         {
30.             get { return PlayReadyStatics.PlayReadyCertificateSecurityLevel != 0; }
31.         }
32. 
33.         static public MediaProtectionManager InitializeProtectionManager(ServiceRequestedEventHandler serviceRequestHandler)
34.         {
35. 
36.             var protectionManager = new MediaProtectionManager();
37. 
38.             //A setting to tell MF that we are using PlayReady.
39.             var props = new Windows.Foundation.Collections.PropertySet();
40.             props.Add("{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}", "Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady.PlayReadyWinRTTrustedInput");
41.             protectionManager.Properties.Add("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemIdMapping", props);
42.             protectionManager.Properties.Add("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemId", "{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}");
43.             //Maps the conatiner guid from the manifest or media segment
44.             protectionManager.Properties.Add("Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionContainerGuid", "{9A04F079-9840-4286-AB92-E65BE0885F95}");
45. 
46.             protectionManager.ServiceRequested += new ServiceRequestedEventHandler(serviceRequestHandler);
47.             protectionManager.ComponentLoadFailed += new ComponentLoadFailedEventHandler(ComponentLoadFailed);
48. 
49.             // Windows 10 provides built in support for Dash and does not require additional configuration. 
50.             // This would be be good place to configure a MediaExtensionManager to support another stream source 
51.             // such as the Smooth Streaming SDK
52. 
53.             return protectionManager;
54. 
55.         }
56. 
57.         static public PlayReadyLicenseSession createLicenseSession()
58.         {
59.             //A setting to tell MF that we are using PlayReady.
60.             var propSetMF = new Windows.Foundation.Collections.PropertySet();
61.             propSetMF["Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemId"] = "{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}";
62.             var cpsystems = new Windows.Foundation.Collections.PropertySet();
63.             cpsystems["{F4637010-03C3-42CD-B932-B48ADF3A6A54}"] = "Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady.PlayReadyWinRTTrustedInput"; //Playready TrustedInput Class Name
64.             propSetMF["Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionSystemIdMapping"] = cpsystems;
65. 
66.             //Create the MF media session that the license will be tied to
67.             var pmpServer = new Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionPMPServer(propSetMF);
68. 
69.             var propSetPMPServer = new Windows.Foundation.Collections.PropertySet();
70.             //Set the property for the LicenseSession. This tells PlayReady to tie the license to that particular media session
71.             propSetPMPServer["Windows.Media.Protection.MediaProtectionPMPServer"] = pmpServer;
72. 
73.             var licenseSession = new Windows.Media.Protection.PlayReady.PlayReadyLicenseSession(propSetPMPServer);
74.             return licenseSession;
75.         }
76. 
77. 
78.         /// <summary>
79.         /// A ComponentLoadFailed event will occur when binary data fails to load.
80.         /// This an infrequent event that may be triggered by a pending OS update in which DRM compoments 
81.         /// are being renewed and may require a restart.
82.         /// </summary>
83.         static void ComponentLoadFailed(MediaProtectionManager sender, ComponentLoadFailedEventArgs e)
84.         {
85. 
86.             for (int i = 0; i < e.Information.Items.Count; i++)
87.             {
88.                 ViewModelBase.Log(e.Information.Items[i].Name + "\nReasons=0x" + e.Information.Items[i].Reasons + "\n"
89.                                                     + "Renewal Id=" + e.Information.Items[i].RenewalId);
90.             }
91.             e.Completion.Complete(false);
92.         }
93. 
94.         /// <summary>
95.         /// Reactive individualization is triggered by PlayReady when the DRM is first utilized in a new app.
96.         /// If individualization was triggered as part a license request then that request will be queued until
97.         /// the indiv process completes. The serviceCompletionNotifier is utilized to notify the ProtectionManager 
98.         /// that the next service request can begin.
99.         /// </summary>
100.         static public async void ReactiveIndividualization(PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest indivRequest,
101.                                                                 MediaProtectionServiceCompletion serviceCompletionNotifier,
102.                                                                 Action callback = null)
103.         {
104.             Exception exception = null;
105.             try
106.             {
107.                 // The actual service call. 
108.                 // The Indiv ServiceRequest call cannot be customized like most other ServiceRequests. 
109.                 await indivRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
110.             }
111.             catch (Exception ex)
112.             {
113.                 exception = ex;
114.             }
115.             finally
116.             {
117.                 if (serviceCompletionNotifier != null)
118.                 {
119.                     // The Complete call will notify listeners that the service call is complete 
120.                     // and queued service requests can begin.
121.                     serviceCompletionNotifier.Complete(exception == null);
122.                 }
123.                 ViewModelBase.Log("ReactiveIndividualization::Complete");
124.             }
125. 
126.             // The callback is only used in the sample to show PlayReadyStatics within the UI on the 
127.             // completion of the ServiceRequest.
128.             if (callback != null)
129.             {
130.                 var dispatcher = CoreApplication.MainView.CoreWindow.Dispatcher;
131.                 await dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
132.                 {
133.                     callback.Invoke();
134.                 });
135.             }
136.         }
137. 
138.         /// <summary>
139.         /// Reactive license acquisition is triggered by PlayReady when the DRM does not have a license for the 
140.         /// content(KeyID) as part of an active playback attempt.
141.         /// Service requests can be queued which is useful in scenarios such as root/leaf license and PlayReady Domains 
142.         /// where one license depends on another. The serviceCompletionNotifier is utilized to notify the ProtectionManager 
143.         /// that the next service request can begin.
144.         /// </summary>
145.         static async public void ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(IPlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest licenseRequest, 
146.                                                                    MediaProtectionServiceCompletion serviceCompletionNotifier,
147.                                                                    Action callback = null)
148.         {
149.             
150.             Exception exception = null;
151.             bool success = false;
152.             try
153.             {
154.                 await licenseRequest.BeginServiceRequest();
155.                 success = true;
156.             }
157.             catch (Exception ex)
158.             {
159.                 exception = ex;
160.             }
161.             finally
162.             {
163.                 if (serviceCompletionNotifier != null)
164.                 {
165.                     // The Complete call will notify listeners that the service call is complete
166.                     // and queued service requests can begin.
167.                     serviceCompletionNotifier.Complete(success);
168.                     serviceCompletionNotifier = null;
169.                 }
170.                 ViewModelBase.Log("ReactiveLicenseAcquisition::Complete");
171.             }
172. 
173.             // optional call back to update UI.
174.             if (callback != null)
175.             {
176.                 var dispatcher = CoreApplication.MainView.CoreWindow.Dispatcher;
177.                 await dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () =>
178.                 {
179.                     callback.Invoke();
180.                 });
181. 
182.             }
183.         }
184. 
185.         /// <summary>
186.         /// The individual process can kick off proactively and not need to wait for a reactionary service
187.         /// request from PlayReady. This enables the app to manage when activation occures.
188.         /// </summary>
189.         static public void ProactiveIndividualization(Action callback)
190.         {
191.             //Creating a new Indiv request and using the previous reactive method to complete the request 
192.             var indivRequest = new PlayReadyIndividualizationServiceRequest();
193.             ReactiveIndividualization(indivRequest, null, callback);
194.         }
195. 
196.         /// <summary>
197.         /// The application can proativly request a license by creating a license request and 
198.         /// assigning a header which provides the specifics such as the KeyId of the license needed.
199.         /// </summary>
200.         static public void ProactiveLicenseAcquisition(PlayReadyContentHeader contentHeader, Action callback = null)
201.         {
202.             var laRequest = new PlayReadyLicenseAcquisitionServiceRequest();
203.             laRequest.ContentHeader = contentHeader;
204.             ReactiveLicenseAcquisition(laRequest, null, callback);
205.         }
206. 
207. 
208. 
209. 
210.     }
211. }

12. using System;
13. using Windows.ApplicationModel.Activation;
14. using Windows.UI.Xaml;
15. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Controls;
16. using Windows.UI.Xaml.Navigation;

18. // The Blank Application template is documented at http://go.microsoft.com/fwlink/?LinkId=402347&clcid=0x409
19. 
20. namespace SDKTemplate
21. {
22.     /// <summary>
23.     /// Provides application-specific behavior to supplement the default Application class.
24.     /// </summary>
25.     sealed partial class App : Application
26.     {
27.         /// <summary>
28.         /// Initializes the singleton application object.  This is the first line of authored code
29.         /// executed, and as such is the logical equivalent of main() or WinMain().
30.         /// </summary>
31.         public App()
32.         {
33.             this.InitializeComponent();
34.             this.Construct();
35.         }
36. 
37.         /// <summary>
38.         /// Invoked when the application is launched normally by the end user.  Other entry points
39.         /// will be used such as when the application is launched to open a specific file.
40.         /// </summary>
41.         /// <param name="e">Details about the launch request and process.</param>
42.         protected override void OnLaunched(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e)
43.         {
44. 
45. #if DEBUG
46.             if (System.Diagnostics.Debugger.IsAttached)
47.             {
48.                 this.DebugSettings.EnableFrameRateCounter = false;
49.             }
50. #endif
51. 
52.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
53. 
54.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
55.             // just ensure that the window is active
56.             if (rootFrame == null)
57.             {
58.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
59.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
60.                 // Set the default language
61.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
62. 
63.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
64. 
65.                 if (e.PreviousExecutionState == ApplicationExecutionState.Terminated)
66.                 {
67.                     //TODO: Load state from previously suspended application
68.                 }
69. 
70.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
71.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
72.             }
73. 
74.             if (rootFrame.Content == null)
75.             {
76.                 // When the navigation stack isn't restored navigate to the first page,
77.                 // configuring the new page by passing required information as a navigation
78.                 // parameter
79.                 rootFrame.Navigate(typeof(MainPage), e.Arguments);
80.             }
81. 
82.             // Ensure the current window is active
83.             Window.Current.Activate();
84. 
85.             // Some samples want access to the LaunchActivatedEventArgs.
86.             LaunchCompleted(e);
87.         }
88. 
89.         private Frame CreateRootFrame()
90.         {
91.             Frame rootFrame = Window.Current.Content as Frame;
92. 
93.             // Do not repeat app initialization when the Window already has content,
94.             // just ensure that the window is active
95.             if (rootFrame == null)
96.             {
97.                 // Create a Frame to act as the navigation context and navigate to the first page
98.                 rootFrame = new Frame();
99. 
100.                 // Set the default language
101.                 rootFrame.Language = Windows.Globalization.ApplicationLanguages.Languages[0];
102.                 rootFrame.NavigationFailed += OnNavigationFailed;
103. 
104.                 // Place the frame in the current Window
105.                 Window.Current.Content = rootFrame;
106.             }
107. 
108.             return rootFrame;
109.         }
110. 
111.         /// <summary>
112.         /// Invoked when Navigation to a certain page fails
113.         /// </summary>
114.         /// <param name="sender">The Frame which failed navigation</param>
115.         /// <param name="e">Details about the navigation failure</param>
116.         void OnNavigationFailed(object sender, NavigationFailedEventArgs e)
117.         {
118.             throw new Exception("Failed to load Page " + e.SourcePageType.FullName);
119.         }
120. 
121.         // Add any application constructor code in here.
122.         partial void Construct();
123. 
124.         // Add any OnLaunched customization here.
125.         partial void LaunchCompleted(LaunchActivatedEventArgs e);
126.     }
127. }

25. {
26.     /// <summary>
27.     /// An empty page that can be used on its own or navigated to within a Frame.
28.     /// </summary>
29.     public sealed partial class MainPage : Page
30.     {
31.         public static MainPage Current;
32. 
33.         public MainPage()
34.         {
35.             this.InitializeComponent();
36. 
37.             // This is a static public property that allows downstream pages to get a handle to the MainPage instance
38.             // in order to call methods that are in this class.
39.             Current = this;
40.             SampleTitle.Text = FEATURE_NAME;
41.         }
42. 
43.         protected override void OnNavigatedTo(NavigationEventArgs e)
44.         {
45.             // Populate the scenario list from the SampleConfiguration.cs file
46.             var itemCollection = new List<Scenario>();
47.             int i = 1;
48.             foreach (Scenario s in scenarios)
49.             {
50.                 itemCollection.Add(new Scenario { Title = $"{i++}) {s.Title}", ClassType = s.ClassType });
51.             }
52.             ScenarioControl.ItemsSource = itemCollection;
53. 
54.             if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
55.             {
56.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = -1;
57.             }
58.             else
59.             {
60.                 ScenarioControl.SelectedIndex = 0;
61.             }
62.         }
63. 
64.         /// <summary>
65.         /// Called whenever the user changes selection in the scenarios list.  This method will navigate to the respective
66.         /// sample scenario page.
67.         /// </summary>
68.         /// <param name="sender"></param>
69.         /// <param name="e"></param>
70.         private void ScenarioControl_SelectionChanged(object sender, SelectionChangedEventArgs e)
71.         {
72.             // Clear the status block when navigating scenarios.
73.             NotifyUser(String.Empty, NotifyType.StatusMessage);
74. 
75.             ListBox scenarioListBox = sender as ListBox;
76.             Scenario s = scenarioListBox.SelectedItem as Scenario;
77.             if (s != null)
78.             {
79.                 ScenarioFrame.Navigate(s.ClassType);
80.                 if (Window.Current.Bounds.Width < 640)
81.                 {
82.                     Splitter.IsPaneOpen = false;
83.                 }
84.             }
85.         }
86. 
87.         public List<Scenario> Scenarios
88.         {
89.             get { return this.scenarios; }
90.         }
91. 
92.         /// <summary>
93.         /// Display a message to the user.
94.         /// This method may be called from any thread.
95.         /// </summary>
96.         /// <param name="strMessage"></param>
97.         /// <param name="type"></param>
98.         public void NotifyUser(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
99.         {
100.             // If called from the UI thread, then update immediately.
101.             // Otherwise, schedule a task on the UI thread to perform the update.
102.             if (Dispatcher.HasThreadAccess)
103.             {
104.                 UpdateStatus(strMessage, type);
105.             }
106.             else
107.             {
108.                 var task = Dispatcher.RunAsync(CoreDispatcherPriority.Normal, () => UpdateStatus(strMessage, type));
109.             }
110.         }
111. 
112.         private void UpdateStatus(string strMessage, NotifyType type)
113.         {
114.             switch (type)
115.             {
116.                 case NotifyType.StatusMessage:
117.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Green);
118.                     break;
119.                 case NotifyType.ErrorMessage:
120.                     StatusBorder.Background = new SolidColorBrush(Windows.UI.Colors.Red);
121.                     break;
122.             }
123. 
124.             StatusBlock.Text = strMessage;
125. 
126.             // Collapse the StatusBlock if it has no text to conserve real estate.
127.             StatusBorder.Visibility = (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty) ? Visibility.Visible : Visibility.Collapsed;
128.             if (StatusBlock.Text != String.Empty)
129.             {
130.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
131.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
132.             }
133.             else
134.             {
135.                 StatusBorder.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
136.                 StatusPanel.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
137.             }
138. 
139. 			// Raise an event if necessary to enable a screen reader to announce the status update.
140. 			var peer = FrameworkElementAutomationPeer.FromElement(StatusBlock);
141. 			if (peer != null)
142. 			{
143. 				peer.RaiseAutomationEvent(AutomationEvents.LiveRegionChanged);
144. 			}
145. 		}
146.

150.         }

155.         }
